30782:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

30841:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
30842:     }catch(e){}

30754:         if(save){
30755:           save.disabled=true;
30756:           save.textContent='جارٍ الحفظ…';

30764:         }
30765:       }
30766:     };
30767:   }
30768: }
30769: function closeModal(){var r=E('rw-hr-modal-root');if(r)r.remove()}
30770:   function ppl(){return H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'}).map(function(e){return{value:e.id,label:(e.name||e.email)+' — '+e.email}})}
30771:   async function loadPeople(){var d=await q('employees');H.employees=d.rows||[];return H.employees}
30772:   async function loadBranches(){var r=await supabase.from('branches').select('id,branch_code,name,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).order('name');if(r.error)throw r.error;H.branches=r.data||[];return H.branches}
30773:   function branches(){return H.branches.filter(function(x){return x.is_active!==false}).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:(x.branch_code||'')+' — '+x.name}})}
30774:   function employeeOpts(){return ppl()}
30775:   function deptOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
30776:   function posOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.title}})}
30777:   function scheduleOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
30778:   function tabbar(){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm p-2 flex gap-2 flex-wrap">'+T.map(function(x){return '<button type="button" data-hr-tab="'+x[0]+'" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black text-sm '+(H.tab===x[0]?'bg-indigo-600 text-white':'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50')+'"><i class="fas '+x[2]+' ml-1"></i>'+x[1]+'</button>'}).join('')+'</div>'}
30779:   function employeeMeta(e){return '<div class="space-y-2 text-sm"><div><span class="text-slate-500">القسم:</span> <b>'+esc(e.department_name||e.department||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الوظيفة:</span> <b>'+esc(e.position_name||e.job_title||e.role||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الفرع:</span> <b>'+esc(e.branch_name||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">العقد:</span> '+(e.contract_status==='active'?badge('فعال','ok'):badge(e.contract_status||'غير موجود','muted'))+'</div></div>'}

30782:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

30818:   (function installModalResilience(){
30819:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
30820:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
30821:       if(!ac)return;
30822:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
30823:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
30824:       e.preventDefault();
30825:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
30826:     },true);
30827:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
30828:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
30829:       if(!root)return;
30830:       e.preventDefault();
30831:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
30832:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
30833:     });
30834:     try{
30835:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
30836:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
30837:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
30838:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
30839:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
30840:       });
30841:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
30842:     }catch(e){}
30843:   }());

28453: function _exportReportCsv() {
28454:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
28455: 
28456:     if (!resultDiv) {
28457:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للتصدير', 'info');
28458:         return;
28459:     }
28460: 
28461:     var table = resultDiv.querySelector('table');
28462: 
28463:     if (!table) {
28464:         _showToast('لا يوجد جدول قابل للتصدير', 'info');
28465:         return;
28466:     }
28467: 
28468:     var rows = table.querySelectorAll('tr');
28469:     var csv = [];
28470: 
28471:     function csvCell(value) {
28472:         var s = String(value == null ? '' : value)
28473:             .replace(/\r?\n|\r/g, ' ')
28474:             .replace(/"/g, '""');
28475: 
28476:         return '"' + s + '"';
28477:     }
28478: 
28479:     for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
28480:         var cells = rows[i].querySelectorAll('th,td');
28481:         var line = [];
28482: 
28483:         for (var j = 0; j < cells.length; j++) {
28484:             line.push(csvCell(cells[j].innerText || ''));
28485:         }
28486: 
28487:         csv.push(line.join(','));
28488:     }
28489: 
28490:     var blob = new Blob(
28491:         ['\uFEFF' + csv.join('\r\n')],
28492:         { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' }
28493:     );
28494: 
28495:     var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
28496:     var a = document.createElement('a');
28497: 
28498:     a.href = url;
28499:     a.download =
28500:         'rawaea-report-' +
28501:         String(_currentReport || 'report') +
28502:         '-' +
28503:         new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) +
28504:         '.csv';
28505: 
28506:     document.body.appendChild(a);
28507:     a.click();
28508:     document.body.removeChild(a);
28509: 
28510:     URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
28511: }
28512: function _printReport() {
28513:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
28514: 
28515:     if (!resultDiv || !resultDiv.innerHTML) {
28516:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للطباعة', 'info');
28517:         return;
28518:     }
28519: 
28520:     var printWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
28521: 
28522:     if (!printWindow) {
28523:         _showToast('الرجاء السماح بالنوافذ المنبثقة', 'warning');
28524:         return;
28525:     }
28526: 
28527:     var html =
28528:         '<!DOCTYPE html>' +
28529:         '<html dir="rtl">' +
28530:         '<head>' +
28531:         '<meta charset="UTF-8">' +
28532:         '<title>تقرير الروائع ERP</title>' +
28533:         '<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cairo:wght@400;700;900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">' +
28534:         '<style>' +
28535:         'body{font-family:Cairo,sans-serif;padding:20px;color:#111827}' +
28536:         'table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:15px}' +
28537:         'th,td{border:1px solid #ddd;padding:8px}' +
28538:         'th{background:#f2f2f2;font-weight:800}' +
28539:         '.text-center{text-align:center}' +
28540:         '</style>' +
28541:         '</head>' +
28542:         '<body>' +
28543:         resultDiv.innerHTML +
28544:         '<script>window.onload=function(){window.print();};<\/script>' +
28545:         '</body></html>';
28546: 
28547:     printWindow.document.open();
28548:     printWindow.document.write(html);
28549:     printWindow.document.close();
28550: }

30700: var RW_HR = (function() {

30707:   ];
30708:   function E(id){return typeof byId==='function'?byId(id):document.getElementById(id)}
30709:   function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/\"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#39;')}
30710:   function num(v){v=Number(v);return isFinite(v)?v:0}

30712:   function date(v){return v?String(v).slice(0,10).split('-').reverse().join('/'):'-'}
30713:   function iso(v){return v?new Date(v).toISOString():null}

30715:   function safe(el,html){if(!el)return;if(typeof safeHTML==='function')safeHTML(el,html);else el.innerHTML=html}

30717:   function opClear(k){if(k)delete H.ops[k]}
30718:   async function actor(){var a=await supabase.auth.getUser();if(a.error||!a.data.user)throw Error('جلسة المستخدم غير صالحة');var u=await supabase.from('users').select('id,email,company_id,role,name,status,phone,employee_id,default_branch_id,active_warehouse_role').eq('auth_id',a.data.user.id).maybeSingle();if(u.error)throw u.error;if(!u.data||!u.data.id||!u.data.company_id)throw Error('تعذر تحديد سياق الموظف والشركة');H.actor=u.data;H.companyId=u.data.company_id}
30719:   async function q(view,payload){var r=await supabase.rpc('hr_query',{p_view:view,p_payload:payload||{}});if(r.error)throw r.error;if(!r.data||r.data.success===false)throw Error((r.data&&(r.data.msg||r.data.code))||'فشل قراءة HR');return r.data}
30720:   async function c(command,payload,key){var k=key||('cmd:'+command);var r=await supabase.rpc('hr_command_atomic',{p_command:command,p_payload:payload||{},p_operation_id:opKey(k),p_actor_user_id:H.actor.id,p_actor_email:H.actor.email});if(r.error)throw r.error;if(!r.data||r.data.success===false)throw Error((r.data&&(r.data.msg||r.data.code))||'فشل تنفيذ أمر HR');opClear(k);return r.data}

30723:   function card(title,sub,body,actions){return '<section class="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm overflow-hidden"><div class="px-6 py-5 bg-slate-50/80 border-b flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-3"><div><h3 class="font-black text-slate-800">'+esc(title)+'</h3><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">'+esc(sub||'')+'</p></div><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">'+(actions||'')+'</div></div><div class="p-6">'+body+'</div></section>'}

30725:   function table(headers,rows){if(!rows||!rows.length)return '<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لا توجد بيانات</div>';return '<div class="overflow-auto"><table class="min-w-full text-sm"><thead><tr>'+headers.map(function(h){return '<th class="px-4 py-3 text-right bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 font-black whitespace-nowrap">'+esc(h)+'</th>'}).join('')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.join('')+'</tbody></table></div>'}

30781:   async function employeesTab(cn){await loadPeople();var rows=H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'});cn.innerHTML=card('دليل الموظفين','Employee 360 من مركز واحد','<div class="flex gap-2 mb-5"><input id="hr-emp-search" class="flex-1 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border" placeholder="بحث بالاسم أو البريد أو الرقم أو الوظيفة">'+btn('ملف موظف','new-profile')+'</div><div id="hr-emp-grid" class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4">'+rows.map(function(e){var total=num(e.basic_salary)+num(e.housing_allowance)+num(e.transport_allowance)+num(e.other_allowance)-num(e.default_deduction);return '<article data-eid="'+esc(e.id)+'" class="p-5 bg-white border border-slate-100 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md"><div class="flex items-center gap-3"><div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-black">'+esc((e.name||'?')[0])+'</div><div class="min-w-0"><div class="font-black truncate">'+esc(e.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">'+esc(e.position_name||e.job_title||e.role||'-')+'</div></div></div><div class="mt-4">'+employeeMeta(e)+'</div><div class="mt-4 pt-3 border-t flex justify-between text-sm"><span class="text-slate-500">التعويض الحالي</span><b class="text-indigo-700">'+money(total)+' EGP</b></div></article>'}).join('')+'</div>');var s=E('hr-emp-search');if(s)s.oninput=function(){var v=s.value.toLowerCase();cn.querySelectorAll('[data-eid]').forEach(function(el){var e=rows.filter(function(x){return x.id===el.getAttribute('data-eid')})[0]||{};var h=[e.name,e.email,e.employee_number,e.job_title,e.department_name,e.position_name].join(' ').toLowerCase();el.style.display=!v||h.indexOf(v)>-1?'':'none'})};cn.querySelectorAll('[data-eid]').forEach(function(el){el.onclick=function(){open360(el.getAttribute('data-eid'))}})}

30817:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
30818:   (function installModalResilience(){
30819:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
30820:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
30821:       if(!ac)return;
30822:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
30823:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
30824:       e.preventDefault();
30825:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
30826:     },true);
30827:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
30828:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
30829:       if(!root)return;
30830:       e.preventDefault();
30831:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
30832:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
30833:     });
30834:     try{
30835:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
30836:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
30837:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
30838:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
30839:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
30840:       });
30841:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
30842:     }catch(e){}
30843:   }());

30851: var RW_CRM = (function() {
30852:     'use strict';
30853: 
30854:     var state = {
30855:         customers: [],
30856:         assignees: [],
30857:         kpi: {},
30858:         search: '',
30859:         activeOnly: false,
30860:         searchTimer: null
30861:     };
30862: 
30863:     function _esc(s) {
30864:         return String(s == null ? '' : s)
30865:             .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
30866:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
30867:             .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
30868:             .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
30869:             .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
30870:     }
30871: 
30872:     function _fmtNum(n) {
30873:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG');
30874:     }
30875: 
30876:     function _fmtMoney(n) {
30877:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG') + ' EGP';
30878:     }
30879: 
30880:     function _today() {
30881:         var d = new Date();
30882:         var local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
30883:         return local.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
30884:     }
30885: 
30886:     function _statusLabel(s) {
30887:         var map = {
30888:             Open: 'مفتوحة',
30889:             'معلقة': 'معلقة',
30890:             completed: 'مكتملة',
30891:             'مكتملة': 'مكتملة',
30892:             cancelled: 'ملغاة',
30893:             'ملغاة': 'ملغاة'
30894:         };
30895:         return map[s] || s || 'غير محددة';
30896:     }
30897: 
30898:     function _statusClass(s) {
30899:         if (s === 'completed' || s === 'مكتملة') return 'bg-green-100 text-green-700';
30900:         if (s === 'cancelled' || s === 'ملغاة') return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600';
30901:         return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700';
30902:     }
30903: 
30904:     function _assignedLabel(customer) {
30905:         var rows = Array.isArray(customer && customer.assigned_to) ? customer.assigned_to : [];
30906:         if (!rows.length) return 'غير مسند';
30907:         var active = rows.filter(function(x) { return x && x.active !== false; });
30908:         if (!active.length) active = rows;
30909:         return active.slice(0, 2).map(function(x) {
30910:             return x.name || x.email || '—';
30911:         }).join('، ') + (active.length > 2 ? ' +' + (active.length - 2) : '');
30912:     }
30913: 
30914:     async function _loadDirectory() {
30915:         var res = await supabase.rpc('crm_customer_directory', {
30916:             p_search: state.search || null,
30917:             p_active_only: state.activeOnly,
30918:             p_limit: 200,
30919:             p_offset: 0
30920:         });
30921:         if (res.error) throw res.error;
30922: 
30923:         var payload = res.data || {};
30924:         state.customers = Array.isArray(payload.customers) ? payload.customers : [];
30925:         state.assignees = Array.isArray(payload.assignees) ? payload.assignees : [];
30926:         state.kpi = payload.kpi || {};
30927:         return payload;
30928:     }
30929: 
30930:     function _renderKpis() {
30931:         var k = state.kpi || {};
30932:         return '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">' +
30933:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">إجمالي العملاء</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.total_customers) + '</div></div>' +
30934:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">عملاء نشطون</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-green-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.active_customers) + '</div></div>' +
30935:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">ذمم مسجلة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-red-600 mt-2">' + _fmtMoney(k.master_debt_total) + '</div></div>' +
30936:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متابعات مفتوحة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-amber-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.open_followups) + '</div></div>' +
30937:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متأخرة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-rose-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.overdue_followups) + '</div></div>' +
30938:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">مستحقة اليوم</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-blue-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.due_today) + '</div></div>' +
30939:         '</div>';
30940:     }
30941: 
30942:     function _renderTable() {
30943:         if (!state.customers.length) {
30944:             return '<div class="text-center py-16 text-gray-400"><div class="text-5xl mb-3">👥</div><div class="font-black text-lg">لا توجد عملاء مطابقون</div><div class="text-sm mt-2">غيّر البحث أو الفلاتر ثم أعد المحاولة.</div></div>';
30945:         }
30946: 
30947:         var html = '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">' +
30948:             '<thead class="bg-slate-50"><tr>' +
30949:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">العميل</th>' +
30950:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التواصل</th>' +
30951:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التصنيف</th>' +
30952:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المبيعات</th>' +
30953:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الأوردرات</th>' +
30954:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المتابعة القادمة</th>' +
30955:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">المسؤول</th>' +
30956:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الإجراء</th>' +
30957:             '</tr></thead><tbody>';
30958: 
30959:         for (var i = 0; i < state.customers.length; i++) {
30960:             var c = state.customers[i] || {};
30961:             var overdue = Number(c.overdue_followups || 0) > 0;
30962:             var next = c.next_followup_date ? String(c.next_followup_date) : '—';
30963: 
30964:             html += '<tr class="border-b hover:bg-slate-50">' +
30965:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-black">' + _esc(c.name) + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.customer_code) + '</div></td>' +
30966:                 '<td class="p-3"><div>' + _esc(c.phone || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.area || '—') + '</div></td>' +
30967:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-bold">' + _esc(c.customer_type || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.payment_type || '—') + '</div></td>' +
30968:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtMoney(c.sales_total) + '</td>' +
30969:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtNum(c.order_count) + '</td>' +
30970:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black ' + (overdue ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700') + '">' + _esc(next) + '</span></td>' +
30971:                 '<td class="p-3">' + _esc(_assignedLabel(c)) + '</td>' +
30972:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><button type="button" data-crm-open360="' + _esc(c.id) + '" class="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-black">متابعة</button></td>' +
30973:             '</tr>';
30974:         }
30975: 
30976:         return html + '</tbody></table></div>';
30977:     }
30978: 
30979:     function _bindDirectory() {
30980:         var search = byId('crm-search');
30981:         if (search) {
30982:             search.value = state.search;
30983:             search.addEventListener('input', function() {
30984:                 state.search = search.value.trim();
30985:                 clearTimeout(state.searchTimer);
30986:                 state.searchTimer = setTimeout(function() {
30987:                     _loadDirectory().then(function() {
30988:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-customers-list'), _renderTable());
30989:                         _bindOpen360();
30990:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-kpis'), _renderKpis());
30991:                     }).catch(function(e) {
30992:                         showToast(e.message || 'فشل البحث', 'error');
30993:                     });
30994:                 }, 250);
30995:             });
30996:         }

30816:   function realtime(){try{if(H.channel)supabase.removeChannel(H.channel);var tables=['employee_profiles','employee_attendance','employee_leave_requests','employee_documents','hr_departments','hr_positions','hr_employee_assignments','hr_employee_schedule_assignments','hr_work_schedules','hr_attendance_events','hr_work_entries','hr_leave_types','hr_leave_balances','hr_requests','hr_request_approvals','hr_salary_advances','hr_salary_components','hr_contracts','hr_contract_components','hr_payroll_periods','hr_payroll_runs','hr_payslips','hr_payslip_lines','hr_payroll_accounting_map'];H.channel=supabase.channel('rw-hr-mother-final');tables.forEach(function(t){H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'*',schema:'public',table:t},function(){clearTimeout(H.timer);H.timer=setTimeout(function(){render()},700)})});H.channel.subscribe()}catch(e){console.warn('RW_HR realtime',e)}}
30817:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
30818:   (function installModalResilience(){
30819:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
30820:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
30821:       if(!ac)return;
30822:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
30823:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
30824:       e.preventDefault();
30825:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
30826:     },true);
30827:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
30828:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
30829:       if(!root)return;
30830:       e.preventDefault();
30831:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
30832:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
30833:     });
30834:     try{
30835:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
30836:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
30837:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
30838:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
30839:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
30840:       });
30841:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
30842:     }catch(e){}
30843:   }());
